16234 +                     Vec::new()

16237 +                     // shard index is cheaper than a HashMap for the small

16239 +                     let mut per_shard: Vec<Vec<(MerkleHash, crab_xet::xorb::format::XorbRef)>> =

16244 +                     // multiple paths in the tree would produce duplicate
16245 +                     // chunk entries across the per-shard buckets.

16250 +                         if !seen_files.insert(file_hash) {
16251 +                             continue;
16252 +                         }

16254 +                             // Remote-only pointers (verified durable by step 2)
16255 ~                             // have no shard entry — skip them here; their
16256 +                             // chunks are already durable elsewhere.
16257 +                             continue;
16258 +                         };

16260 +                             // Defense in depth: log and skip rather than panic.
16261 +                             warn!(

16279 ~                                     // chunk has a placement. Log and skip — the
16280 +                                     // push already succeeded at this point so we
16281 +                                     // are defensive about cleanup warnings.
16282 +                                     return Ok(());

1641 +                 // Split the ordered push batch into real specs (which
1642 +                 // flow into the pipeline) and parse-time rejections
1643 +                 // (which short-circuit to the response map). The
1644 +                 // original `items` ordering drives response emission
1645 +                 // so clients see `ok`/`error` lines in the same order
1646 +                 // they pushed.
1647 +                 let mut specs: Vec<PushSpec> = Vec::with_capacity(items.len());
1648 +                 let mut pre_rejected: Vec<(String, PushRejectReason)> = Vec::new();
1649 +                 // Synthetic spec sequence preserving per-line order so
1650 +                 // `format_push_response` emits one line per original
1651 +                 // `push` input. Pre-rejected entries use an empty
1652 +                 // `src` since they never reach the pipeline.
1653 +                 let mut ordered_specs: Vec<PushSpec> = Vec::with_capacity(items.len());
1654 +                 for item in items {
1655 +                     match item {
1656 +                         PushItem::Spec(spec) => {
1657 +                             specs.push(spec.clone());
1658 +                             ordered_specs.push(spec.clone());
1659 +                         }
1660 +                         PushItem::Rejected { dst, reason } => {
1661 +                             pre_rejected.push((dst.clone(), reason.clone()));
1662 +                             ordered_specs.push(PushSpec {
1663 +                                 force: false,
1664 +                                 src: String::new(),
1665 +                                 dst: dst.clone(),
1666 +                             });
1667 +                         }
1668 +                     }
1669 +                 }
1670 + 
1671 +                 if options.dry_run {
1672 +                     // Git has already checked advertised refs, ancestry, and
1673 +                     // leases. Like send-pack's dry-run, these are previews,
1674 +                     // not receipts: do not prepare auth sessions or acquire locks.
1675 +                     let blocker = pre_rejected.first().filter(|_| options.atomic);
1676 +                     let mut result =
1677 +                         duplicate_destination_result(&ordered_specs).unwrap_or_else(|| {
1678 +                             PushResult::new(
1679 +                                 specs
1680 +                                     .iter()
1681 +                                     .map(|spec| {
1682 +                                         let outcome = match blocker {
1683 +                                             Some((dst, _)) => RefPushOutcome::Rejected(
1684 +                                                 PushRejectReason::AtomicAbort {
1685 +                                                     blocked_by: dst.clone(),
1686 +                                                 },
1687 +                                             ),
1688 +                                             None => RefPushOutcome::Ok,
1689 +                                         };
1690 +                                         (spec.dst.clone(), outcome)
1691 +                                     })
1692 +                                     .collect(),
1693 +                             )
1694 +                         });
1695 +                     for (dst, reason) in pre_rejected {
1696 +                         result
1697 +                             .outcomes
1698 +                             .insert(dst, RefPushOutcome::Rejected(reason));
1699 +                     }
1700 +                     writer
1701 +                         .write_all(format_push_response(&result, &ordered_specs).as_bytes())
1702 +                         .await?;
1703 +                     writer.flush().await?;
1704 +                     return Ok(());
1705 +                 }
1706 + 
1707 +                 let config = cache.config().clone();
1708 +                 let mut base_config = PushConfig::from_config(&config);
1709 +                 let mut push_setup_error = if specs.is_empty() {
1710 +                     None
1711 +                 } else {
1712 +                     configure_active_active_push_coordinator(
1713 +                         &config,
1714 +                         remote_url,
1715 +                         prefix,
1716 +                         &mut base_config,
1717 +                     )
1718 +                     .await
1719 +                     .err()
1720 +                 };
1721 +                 let mut native_config = native_push_config_for_helper(
1722 +                     base_config,
1723 +                     options,
1724 +                     progress_mode,
1725 +                     jsonl_stderr_stream,
1726 +                 );
1727 +                 let mut push_store = store.cloned();
1728 +                 if !specs.is_empty() && push_setup_error.is_none() {
1729 +                     if let Some(repository) = managed_repository {
1730 +                         match store {
1731 +                             Some(read_store) => {
1732 +                                 match crate::git::protected_push::prepare_managed_push(
1733 +                                     &config,
1734 +                                     repository,
1735 +                                     read_store,
1736 +                                     prefix,
1737 +                                     &specs,
1738 +                                     staging.reader(),
1739 +                                     cancel,
1740 +                                 )
1741 +                                 .await
1742 +                                 {
1743 +                                     Ok(protected) => {
1744 +                                         native_config.push.atomic = true;
1745 +                                         native_config.push.protected_push = Some(protected.session);
1746 +                                         push_store = Some(protected.store);
1747 +                                     }
1748 +                                     Err(error) => push_setup_error = Some(error),
1749 +                                 }
1750 +                             }
1751 +                             None => {
1752 +                                 push_setup_error = Some(CrabError::Configuration {
1753 +                                     key: "managed push store".to_owned(),
1754 +                                     origin:
1755 +                                         "managed protected push requires its resolved read store"
1756 +                                             .to_owned(),
1757 +                                 });
1758 +                             }
1759 +                         }
1760 +                     } else if matches!(
1761 +                         config.auth.provider,
1762 +                         crate::core::config::AuthProvider::CrabAuth
1763 +                     ) {
1764 +                         match remote_url
1765 +                             .ok_or_else(|| CrabError::Protocol("missing remote URL".into()))
1766 +                             .and_then(crate::git::url::CrabUrl::parse)
1767 +                         {
1768 +                             Ok(parsed) => match crate::git::protected_push::prepare_crab_auth_push(
1769 +                                 &config, &parsed, &specs, cancel,
1770 +                             )
1771 +                             .await
1772 +                             {
1773 +                                 Ok(protected) => {
1774 +                                     native_config.push.atomic = true;
1775 +                                     native_config.push.protected_push = Some(protected.session);
1776 +                                     push_store = Some(protected.store);
1777 +                                 }
1778 +                                 Err(e) => push_setup_error = Some(e),
1779 +                             },
1780 +                             Err(e) => push_setup_error = Some(e),
1781 +                         }
1782 +                     }
1783 +                 }
1784 + 
1785 +                 let router = if let Some(s) = push_store.as_ref() {
1786 +                     StoreLayout::new(s.clone(), prefix.to_owned())
1787 +                 } else {
1788 +                     StoreLayout::new(
1789 +                         crate::storage::store::Store::new(std::sync::Arc::new(
1790 +                             object_store::memory::InMemory::new(),
1791 +                         )),
1792 +                         prefix.to_owned(),
1793 +                     )
1794 +                 };
1795 + 
1796 +                 // Parse-time rejections participate in the atomic contract;
1797 +                 // valid siblings must not reach a pipeline that could commit.
1798 +                 let atomic_parse_blocker = if native_config.push.atomic {
1799 +                     pre_rejected.first().map(|(dst, _)| dst.clone())
1800 +                 } else {
1801 +                     None
1802 +                 };
1803 +                 let mut result = if let Some(blocked_by) = atomic_parse_blocker {
1804 +                     let outcomes = specs
1805 +                         .iter()
1806 +                         .map(|spec| {
1807 +                             (
1808 +                                 spec.dst.clone(),
1809 +                                 RefPushOutcome::Rejected(PushRejectReason::AtomicAbort {
1810 +                                     blocked_by: blocked_by.clone(),
1811 +                                 }),
1812 +                             )
1813 +                         })
1814 +                         .collect();
1815 +                     PushResult::new(outcomes)
1816 +                 } else if specs.is_empty() {
1817 +                     PushResult::empty()
1818 +                 } else if let Some(e) = push_setup_error {
1819 +                     tracing::error!(error = %e, "push setup failed");
1820 +                     reject_specs_for_error(&specs, &e)
1821 +                 } else {
1822 +                     let push_store =
1823 +                         push_store
1824 +                             .as_ref()
1825 +                             .ok_or_else(|| CrabError::Configuration {
1826 +                                 key: "capsule-protocol push store".to_owned(),
1827 +                                 origin: "push requires a resolved object store".to_owned(),
1828 +                             })?;
1829 +                     match crate::git::capsule_push::run(
1830 +                         &native_config.push,
1831 +                         &specs,
1832 +                         push_store,
1833 +                         &router,
1834 +                         cache.capsule_ref_view.take(),
1835 +                         &config.transfer_hide_refs,
1836 +                         staging.reader(),
1837 +                         caching_store,
1838 +                         Some(cache.metrics.as_ref()),
1839 +                         cancel,
1840 +                     )
1841 +                     .await
1842 +                     {
1843 +                         Ok((r, view)) => {
1844 +                             cache.capsule_ref_view = view;
1845 +                             r
1846 +                         }
1847 +                         // Partial outcomes carry per-ref state the pipeline
1848 ~                         // already computed — unwrap so siblings keep the
1849 +                         // outcomes they earned instead of collapsing to the
1850 +                         // aggregate error string below.
1851 +                         Err(CrabError::PushPartialOutcome { outcomes, .. }) => *outcomes,
1852 +                         Err(e) => {
1853 +                             // Structured per-ref rejection for truly batch-
1854 +                             // global failures (protocol, cancellation,
1855 +                             // store open). Using the structured `Internal`
1856 +                             // variant keeps the `error {ref} internal`
1857 +                             // response shape and preserves the stringified
1858 +                             // chain for logs while dropping the old
1859 +                             // `Error(String)` shape.
1860 +                             tracing::error!(error = %e, "push pipeline failed");
1861 +                             reject_specs_for_error(&specs, &e)
1862 +                         }
1863 +                     }
1864 +                 };
1865 + 
1866 +                 // Merge parse-time rejections into the outcome map.
1867 +                 // Pipeline outcomes never key on a pre-rejected `dst`
1868 +                 // (those specs were filtered out before
1869 +                 // `run_native_push`), so there is no collision to
1870 ~                 // resolve — a simple insert preserves both sides.
1871 +                 for (dst, reason) in pre_rejected {
1872 +                     result
1873 +                         .outcomes
1874 +                         .insert(dst, RefPushOutcome::Rejected(reason));
1875 +                 }
1876 +                 if let Err(err) = record_push_audit_event(
1877 +                     &default_log_path(),
1878 +                     remote_url,
1879 +                     prefix,
1880 +                     &ordered_specs,
1881 +                     &result,
1882 +                     None,
1883 +                 ) {
1884 +                     warn!(%err, "failed to append push audit event");
1885 +                 }
1886 + 
1887 +                 if let Err(error) = cache.persist_pending_metrics() {
1888 +                     warn!(%error, "failed to persist performance counters");
1889 +                 }
1890 + 
1891 +                 let response = format_push_response(&result, &ordered_specs);
1892 +                 writer.write_all(response.as_bytes()).await?;
1893 +                 writer.flush().await?;
1894 +                 if progress_mode == OutputMode::Jsonl
1895 +                     && let Some(stream) = jsonl_stderr_stream
1896 +                     && let Ok(mut s) = stream.lock()
1897 +                 {
1898 +                     // Git already received the authoritative ref outcomes on stdout.
1899 +                     // Optional stderr diagnostics must not turn that reply into failure.
1900 +                     let output = s.emit_result(build_push_result_event(&result, &ordered_specs));
1901 +                     crate::core::output::report_progress_output(output);
1902 +                 }
1903 +                 Ok::<(), CrabError>(())
1904 +             }
